- **disable-review**: Temporarily turn off the requirement that pending changes be reviewed before committing in the current herdr workspace. ([LandonSchropp/agent-toolkit](https://github.com/LandonSchropp/agent-toolkit/tree/HEAD/plugins/ls-interactivity/skills/disable-review) · ★3)
- **disable-review-bot**: Disable (or re-enable) the Claude PR review bot on the current repository — the emergency stop when the reviewer misbehaves, spams verdicts, burns Actions minutes, or blocks merges. Use when someone says "turn off the review bot", "disable claude review", "the reviewer is broken", "stop the bot reviewing PRs", or asks to re-enable it after a pause. ([sentfutures/devops](https://github.com/sentfutures/devops/tree/HEAD/plugins/review-bot/skills/disable-review-bot))
- **disable-rings**: Mute the iPhone ring from the agent CLI, one switch every ring obeys (`hal2-cli-ring mode`). `/disable-rings` = off (no Find My call), `silent [--for 2h]` (the message without sound), `off --for 2h`, `on` the way back, `status`. Use when the user says /disable-rings, "disable rings", "sauna", "mute the iPhone ring", "rings back on". `/disable-rings h` shows help. ([divramod/agent-plugins](https://github.com/divramod/agent-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/divramod-skills/skills/disable-rings))
- **disable-routing**: Switch Codex back to its normal provider for future sessions. ([weave-os/router](https://github.com/weave-os/router/tree/HEAD/install/codex-skills/disable-routing) · ★5.6k · 4 copies)
- **disable-stylus-handwriting**: Brings up the full on-screen keyboard on an Android emulator when a focused text field shows Gboard's stylus handwriting toolbar instead. Use when the toolbar appears but the task needs the on-screen keyboard. ([itsramiel/Software-Engineering](https://github.com/itsramiel/Software-Engineering/tree/HEAD/skills/android/disable-stylus-handwriting))
- **disable-telegram**: Stop the Telegram channel daemon. ([oimiragieo/agent-studio](https://github.com/oimiragieo/agent-studio/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/disable-telegram) · ★45 · 2 copies)
- **disable-test**: Guide for disabling Chromium tests (C++, Android Java, or WebUI). Use this skill when you need to disable a failing or flaky test case. ([chromium/chromium](https://github.com/chromium/chromium/tree/HEAD/agents/skills/disable-test) · ★25k · 6 copies)
